### tick intervals again | dialogs: []
<div data-v="" class="content">
<p class="alert alert-info">
<svg class="svg-inline--fa fa-circle-info me-2" data-prefix="fas" data-icon="circle-info" role="img" viewBox="0 0 512 512" aria-hidden="true">
<path class="" fill="currentColor" d="M256 512a256 256 0 1 0 0-512 256 256 0 1 0 0 512zM224 160a32 32 0 1 1 64 0 32 32 0 1 1 -64 0zm-8 64l48 0c13.3 0 24 10.7 24 24l0 88 8 0c13.3 0 24 10.7 24 24s-10.7 24-24 24l-80 0c-13.3 0-24-10.7-24-24s10.7-24 24-24l24 0 0-64-24 0c-13.3 0-24-10.7-24-24s10.7-24 24-24z">
</path>
</svg>The headings stay on top while the rows scroll in a box of 360px (<code>stickyHeader</code>, <code>scrollHeight</code>). Region stays at the start and Total at the end while the months scroll sideways (<code>pin</code>); the checkboxes before Region stay with it.</p>

### sort it the other way | dialogs: []
<div data-v="" class="content">
<p class="alert alert-info">
<svg class="svg-inline--fa fa-circle-info me-2" data-prefix="fas" data-icon="circle-info" role="img" viewBox="0 0 512 512" aria-hidden="true">
<path class="" fill="currentColor" d="M256 512a256 256 0 1 0 0-512 256 256 0 1 0 0 512zM224 160a32 32 0 1 1 64 0 32 32 0 1 1 -64 0zm-8 64l48 0c13.3 0 24 10.7 24 24l0 88 8 0c13.3 0 24 10.7 24 24s-10.7 24-24 24l-80 0c-13.3 0-24-10.7-24-24s10.7-24 24-24l24 0 0-64-24 0c-13.3 0-24-10.7-24-24s10.7-24 24-24z">
</path>
</svg>The headings stay on top while the rows scroll in a box of 360px (<code>stickyHeader</code>, <code>scrollHeight</code>). Region stays at the start and Total at the end while the months scroll sideways (<code>pin</code>); the checkboxes before Region stay with it.</p>
<div data-v="" class="table-wrapper quintable">
<div data-v="" class="header slot slot-header quintable--header">
</div>
<div data-v="" class="slot slot-after-search quintable--after-search-container">
</div>
<div data-v="" class="clearfix quintable--table-container quintable-scroll quintable-scroll-x" style="max-height: 360px;">
<table data-v="" class="vue-quintable table quintable--table-container--table quintable--sticky-header" style="--quintable-pin-start-0: 0px; --quintable-pin-start-1: 29px; --quintable-pin-end-0: 0px;">

### sort the second sortable column | dialogs: []
<div data-v="" class="content">
<p class="alert alert-info">
<svg class="svg-inline--fa fa-circle-info me-2" data-prefix="fas" data-icon="circle-info" role="img" viewBox="0 0 512 512" aria-hidden="true">
<path class="" fill="currentColor" d="M256 512a256 256 0 1 0 0-512 256 256 0 1 0 0 512zM224 160a32 32 0 1 1 64 0 32 32 0 1 1 -64 0zm-8 64l48 0c13.3 0 24 10.7 24 24l0 88 8 0c13.3 0 24 10.7 24 24s-10.7 24-24 24l-80 0c-13.3 0-24-10.7-24-24s10.7-24 24-24l24 0 0-64-24 0c-13.3 0-24-10.7-24-24s10.7-24 24-24z">
</path>
</svg>The headings stay on top while the rows scroll in a box of 360px (<code>stickyHeader</code>, <code>scrollHeight</code>). Region stays at the start and Total at the end while the months scroll sideways (<code>pin</code>); the checkboxes before Region stay with it.</p>
<div data-v="" class="table-wrapper quintable">
<div data-v="" class="header slot slot-header quintable--header">
</div>
<div data-v="" class="slot slot-after-search quintable--after-search-container">

### toggle the first row's checkbox | dialogs: []
<div data-v="" class="content">
<p class="alert alert-info">
<svg class="svg-inline--fa fa-circle-info me-2" data-prefix="fas" data-icon="circle-info" role="img" viewBox="0 0 512 512" aria-hidden="true">
<path class="" fill="currentColor" d="M256 512a256 256 0 1 0 0-512 256 256 0 1 0 0 512zM224 160a32 32 0 1 1 64 0 32 32 0 1 1 -64 0zm-8 64l48 0c13.3 0 24 10.7 24 24l0 88 8 0c13.3 0 24 10.7 24 24s-10.7 24-24 24l-80 0c-13.3 0-24-10.7-24-24s10.7-24 24-24l24 0 0-64-24 0c-13.3 0-24-10.7-24-24s10.7-24 24-24z">
</path>
</svg>The headings stay on top while the rows scroll in a box of 360px (<code>stickyHeader</code>, <code>scrollHeight</code>). Region stays at the start and Total at the end while the months scroll sideways (<code>pin</code>); the checkboxes before Region stay with it.</p>
<div data-v="" class="table-wrapper quintable">
<div data-v="" class="header slot slot-header quintable--header">

### click the first row | dialogs: []
<div data-v="" class="content">
<p class="alert alert-info">
<svg class="svg-inline--fa fa-circle-info me-2" data-prefix="fas" data-icon="circle-info" role="img" viewBox="0 0 512 512" aria-hidden="true">
<path class="" fill="currentColor" d="M256 512a256 256 0 1 0 0-512 256 256 0 1 0 0 512zM224 160a32 32 0 1 1 64 0 32 32 0 1 1 -64 0zm-8 64l48 0c13.3 0 24 10.7 24 24l0 88 8 0c13.3 0 24 10.7 24 24s-10.7 24-24 24l-80 0c-13.3 0-24-10.7-24-24s10.7-24 24-24l24 0 0-64-24 0c-13.3 0-24-10.7-24-24s10.7-24 24-24z">
</path>
</svg>The headings stay on top while the rows scroll in a box of 360px (<code>stickyHeader</code>, <code>scrollHeight</code>). Region stays at the start and Total at the end while the months scroll sideways (<code>pin</code>); the checkboxes before Region stay with it.</p>
<div data-v="" class="table-wrapper quintable">
<div data-v="" class="header slot slot-header quintable--header">

### click the first row again | dialogs: []
<div data-v="" class="content">
<p class="alert alert-info">
<svg class="svg-inline--fa fa-circle-info me-2" data-prefix="fas" data-icon="circle-info" role="img" viewBox="0 0 512 512" aria-hidden="true">
<path class="" fill="currentColor" d="M256 512a256 256 0 1 0 0-512 256 256 0 1 0 0 512zM224 160a32 32 0 1 1 64 0 32 32 0 1 1 -64 0zm-8 64l48 0c13.3 0 24 10.7 24 24l0 88 8 0c13.3 0 24 10.7 24 24s-10.7 24-24 24l-80 0c-13.3 0-24-10.7-24-24s10.7-24 24-24l24 0 0-64-24 0c-13.3 0-24-10.7-24-24s10.7-24 24-24z">
</path>
</svg>The headings stay on top while the rows scroll in a box of 360px (<code>stickyHeader</code>, <code>scrollHeight</code>). Region stays at the start and Total at the end while the months scroll sideways (<code>pin</code>); the checkboxes before Region stay with it.</p>

### hover the second row | dialogs: []
<div data-v="" class="content">
<p class="alert alert-info">
<svg class="svg-inline--fa fa-circle-info me-2" data-prefix="fas" data-icon="circle-info" role="img" viewBox="0 0 512 512" aria-hidden="true">
<path class="" fill="currentColor" d="M256 512a256 256 0 1 0 0-512 256 256 0 1 0 0 512zM224 160a32 32 0 1 1 64 0 32 32 0 1 1 -64 0zm-8 64l48 0c13.3 0 24 10.7 24 24l0 88 8 0c13.3 0 24 10.7 24 24s-10.7 24-24 24l-80 0c-13.3 0-24-10.7-24-24s10.7-24 24-24l24 0 0-64-24 0c-13.3 0-24-10.7-24-24s10.7-24 24-24z">
</path>
</svg>The headings stay on top while the rows scroll in a box of 360px (<code>stickyHeader</code>, <code>scrollHeight</code>). Region stays at the start and Total at the end while the months scroll sideways (<code>pin</code>); the checkboxes before Region stay with it.</p>
